BENGALURU, WEDNESDAY, 28, FEBRUARY, 2024(PHALGUNA, 09, SHAKAVARSHA, 1945) Volume - 159 Issue 45

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/73/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1289166 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 14-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Domanal Village of Vijayapur taluk
Vijayapur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called R.K
NAGAR and Nama Nagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur
District.
(6012)

included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land
revenue in the village and other head of the account and having regard to the
administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose s to issue a
Notification to alter the limits of Kamanadurga Village of Pavagada Taluk,
Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue villages Bommaganahalli ,

Bhupuru Tanda & Bhupuru as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumkur
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Tumkur District Pavagada Taluk to form a new revenue village called
Bommaganahalli Village of Pavagada Taluk, Tumkur District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 42, KAMGRARA 2018, Dated :
23-11-2018, regarding altering the limits of HIREHALLI village of
Heggadadevanakote Taluk and Mysore District and constituting a new revenue village
called HIREHALLI A CALONY AND TARAKA was published as required by
section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in
the Karnataka Gazette dated : 12-10-2019 inviting objections or suggestions from all
the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Mysore
district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 18-05-2019 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the
areas specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
the said village of Heggadadevanakote Taluk and Mysore District to form a new
revenue village called as HIREHALLI A COLONY of Heggadadevanakote Taluk
and Mysore District The new survey numbers which will form part of this new village
are as specified in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD/61/PFH/2023-RV Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1210820 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date:19-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 160 PFH 2022,
Dated: 10-08-2022 regarding altering the limits of Gowripura village of Gubbi
Taluk in Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as
Thimmappanahatti sub village was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD 160 PFH 2022, Dated: 19-08-2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Tumkuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated :20-08-2022.

And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Thimmappanahatti village of Gubbi
Taluk in Tumakuru District convert the same to form hamlet/ part with in the
boundary of Gowripura revenue village and same will here in after be named as
called Thimmappanahatti of Gowripura village in Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru
District.

05-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Gowripura village of Gubbi Taluk in
Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as
Muttayyanahatti sub village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette RD
211 PFH 2022, Dated: 07-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumkuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 10-11-2022.

And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Muttayyanahatti village of Gubbi Taluk
in Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet/ part with in the boundary of
Gowripura revenue village and same will here in after be named as called
Muttayyanahatti of Gowripura village in Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No: RD 1037 KAMGRARA 2017 Dated:
22-01-2018 regarding altering the limits of Venkatapura village of Pavagada Taluk
and Tumkur District and constituting a new revenue village called Krishnapura and
Srinivasapura was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 1037
KAMGRARA 2017 Dated: 22-03-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from all
the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumkur
district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 15-10-2022 And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Venkatapura of Pavagada taluk in Tumkur District to form a new revenue
village called as Krishnapura of Pavagada taluk Tumkur District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No.RD 351 PFH 2022 dated 7.11.2022
regarding altering the limits of Itkalvillage of Sedam Taluk and Kalaburgi District
and constituting a new revenue sub-village calledAllipur Tanda was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. . 17395 RD 351 PFH 2022 dated 8-11-
2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Kalaburgi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:8-11-2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area Allipur Tanda village of Sedam Taluk of
Kalaburgi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of
Itkal Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Allipur Tanda of
Itkalvillage in Sedam Taluk of KalaburgiDistrict.

regarding altering the limits of Panchehosahalli village of Kadur Taluk in
Chikkamagaluru District and constituting a new revenue village called
Gowdayyanakoppalu, sub village was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD 10 PFH 2023Dated:03.02.2023 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its

district.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on

considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area ofcalled Gowdayyanakoppalu village
Kadur taluk of Chikmagaluru District convert the same to form hamlet/part within
the boundary of Panchehosahalli revenue village and same will here in after be named
as Gowdayyanakoppalu village Kadur taluk, Chikmagaluru district.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Hegadihal Village of Vijayapur taluk
,Vijayapur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Basavanagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Hegadihal Village of Vijayapur taluk ,Vijayapur District to form a new
revenue village called Basavanagar of Vijayapur taluk, Vijayapur District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/52/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1286668 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 14-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Minchanal Village of Vijayapur
taluk Vijayapur District and to constitute a new revenue village called Rama Nagar
as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new
assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur
District.
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Minchanal of Tikota taluk in Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called Rama Nagar of Vijayapur taluk Vijayapur district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 28 PFH 2023, Dated:13-03-2023
regarding altering the limits of Hireboodnur village of Saundatthi Taluk and Belagavi
District and constituting a new revenue village called Kutarnatti was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 429 Part IV-A dated 16-03-2023 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Belagavi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 16-03-2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Saudatthi Taluk and Belagavi District to form a new village called
Kutaranatti of Saudatthi Taluk and Belagavi District. The new survey numbers which
will form part of this new village are as specified in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

E- 1265453 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560 001,
Date:19-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No NO.RD 32 PFH 2021 Dated
20-01-2022 regarding altering the limits of JEEGUNDIPATTANA Village of
Mandya taluk Mandya District and to constitute a new revenue village called B
HOSURU COLONY was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
263 G L H 2022 Dated 25-08-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Mandya district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 24-09-2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village JEEGUNDIPATTANA of Mandya taluk in Mandya .District to form a new
revenue village called B HOSURU COLONY of Mandya taluk Mandya district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the, Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Jakkanahalli Village of
Srirangapattana Taluk of Mandya District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called Srirampur as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Jakkanahalli village for conversion of Srirampura unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or

after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Mandya District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Srirampura village of
Srirangapattana Taluk of Mandya District convert the same to form hamlet/part
within the boundary of Jakkanahalli Revenue village and same will here in after be
named as called as Sri rampura of Jakkanahalli village in Srirangapattana Taluk
of Mandya District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land
revenue in the village and other head of the account and having regard to the
administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose s to issue a
Notification to alter the limits of Venkatapur village of Sedam taluk of
Kalaburagi District to convert the existing Venkatapur Tanda unrecorded
habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue village. The area
occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Venkatapur village for conversion of Venkatapur Tanda unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/ extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner,
Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Venkatapur Thanda village of
Sedam Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension
within the boundary of Venkatapur Revenue village and same will herein after be
named as Venkatapur Tanda of Venkatapur village in Sedam Taluk of Kalaburagi
District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Gunaki Village of Vijayapur taluk
Vijayapur District and to constitute a new revenue village called Minchanal R S as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned
to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur
District.

survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Madlur Village of Yargatti
Taluk, Belagavi District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Benakatti as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and
new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or

suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Belagavi
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of of Yargatti Taluk in Belagavi District to form a new revenue village called
as Benakatti of Yargatti taluk and Belagavi district along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification RD 87 GLH 2022
Dated 16-06-2022 regarding altering the limits of Da-Salapur village of Ramdurg
Taluk and Belagavi district and constituting a new revenue village called Shivan kote
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 87 GLH 2022 dated
16-06-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Belagavi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 28-07-2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village DAADIBHAVI SALAPUR Village of RAMDURG Taluk _ BELAGAVI
District to form a new revenue village called as“SHIVANKOTE” of RAMDURG
Taluk and BELAGAVI District along with the survey numbers included in
column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/204/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1311767 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560 001, Date:09-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 30 PFH 2023, Dated:13-03-2023
regarding altering the limits of Ugargol village of Saundatthi Taluk and Belagavi
District and constituting a new revenue village called Yallamma Tanda was published
as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act
No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 439 Part IV-A dated 17-03-2023 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Belagavi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 17-03-2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Saudatthi Taluk and Belagavi District to form a new village called
Yallamma Tanda of Saudatthi Taluk and Belagavi District. The new survey numbers
which will form part of this new village are as specified in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD149 PFH 2022
Dated: 05-12-2022 regarding altering the limits of Garagadakuppe village of
Tumkur Taluk in Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village called
as Tevaramegalapalya sub village was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD149 PFH 2022 Dated: 06-12-2022 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumkuru
district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 06-01-2023.

And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Tevaramegalapalya village of Tumkur

Taluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form hamlet/part with in the boundary
of Garagadakuppe revenue village and same will here in after be named as called
Tevaramegalapalya of Nagenahalli villagein TumkurTaluk of Tumkur District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 86 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated:
28-09-2018 regarding altering the limits of T.Gollahalli village of Tumkur Taluk in
Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as
Ranganathapura sub village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette RD
86 KAMGRARA 2018, Dated: 08-11-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumkur
district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 15-11-2018.
And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Ranganathapura village of Tumkur
Taluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form hamlet/part within the boundary

of T.Gollahalli revenue village and same will here in after be named as
Ranganathapura of T.Gollahalli village in Tumkur Taluk of Tumkur District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 626 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated 06-9-2017 regarding altering the limits of Vakkoadi Village of Tumkur Taluk,
Tumkur District District and constituting a new revenue village called
Puttayyanapalya was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette 626
KAMGRARA 2017 Dated 06-9-2017 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumkur district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 02-07-2018 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Vakkodi village, Tumkuru Taluk, Tumkuru District to form a new revenue
village called as Puttayyanapalya Tumkuru Taluk, Tumkuru District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.
